float(percentile_inc(col_vals, 0.75))\n\n# Weighted mean row 50 per column\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    cL = col_letter(c)\n    slacks = [computed_values[f\"{cL}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n '/root/output/result_temp.xlsx'\nfinal_path = '/root/output/result.xlsx'\n\n\ndef percentile_inc(values, k: float):\n    if not values:\n        return None\n    xs = sorted(values)\n    n = len(xs)\n    if k <= 0:\n        return float(xs[0])\n    if k >= 1:\n        return float(xs[-1])\n    r = (n - 1) * k + 1\n    lo = int(math.floor(r))\n    hi = int(math.ceil(r))\n    if lo == hi:\n        return float(xs[lo - 1])\n    frac = r - lo\n    x_lo = float(xs[lo - 1])\n    x_hi = float(xs[hi - 1])\n    return x_lo + frac * (x_hi - x_lo)\n\n\ndef median(values):\n    xs = sorted(values)\n    n = len(xs)\n    mid = n // 2\n    if n % 2 == 1:\n        return float(xs[mid])\n    return (float(xs[mid - 1]) + float(xs[mid])) / 2.0\n\n\n# ------------------------\n# Write formulas\n# ------------------------\nwb = openpyxl.load_workbook(src_path)\nws_task = wb['Task']\nws_data = wb['Data']\n\nlookup_row_blocks = [(12, 17), (19, 24), (26, 31)]\n\nfor r0, r1 in lookup_row_blocks:\n    for r in range(r0, r1 + 1):\n        for c in range(8, 13):\n            col = get_column_letter(c)\n            ws_task.cell(r, c).value = (\n                \"=INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38,\"\n                f\"MATCH($D{r},Data!$D$21:$D$38,0),\"\n                f\"MATCH({col}$10,Data!$H$4:$L$4,0))\"\n            )\n\n# Net production slack\nfor i in range(6):\n    slack_row = 35 + i\n    fin_row = 12 + i\n    scrap_row = 19 + i\n    cap_row = 26 + i\n    for c in range(8, 13):\n        col = get_column_letter(c)\n        ws_task.cell(slack_row, c).value = f\"=({col}{fin_row}-{col}{scrap_row})/{col}{cap_row}*100\"\n\n# Stats rows 42-47\nstats = [\n    (42, 'MIN'),\n    (43, 'MAX'),\n    (44, 'MEDIAN'),\n    (45, 'AVERAGE'),\n    (46, 'PERCENTILE', 0.25),\n    (47, 'PERCENTILE', 0.75),\n]\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    col = get_column_letter(c)\n    rng = f\"{col}35:{col}40\"\n    for item in stats:\n        if item[1] == 'PERCENTILE':\n            r, _, k = item\n            ws_task.cell(r, c).value = f\"=PERCENTILE({rng},{k})\"\n        else:\n            r, fn = item\n            ws_task.cell(r, c).value = f\"={fn}({rng})\"\n\n# Weighted mean row 50\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    col = get_column_letter(c)\n    ws_task.cell(50, c).value = f\"=SUMPRODUCT({col}35:{col}40,{col}26:{col}31)/SUM({col}26:{col}31)\"\n\nwb.save(temp_path)\n\n# ------------------------\n# Compute numeric caches\n# ------------------------\n# Build series/year->value map from Data\nDATA_YEAR_ROW = 4\nyears = [ws_data.cell(DATA_YEAR_ROW, c).value for c in range(8, 13)]\nvalue_map = {}\nfor r in range(21, 39):\n    code = ws_data.cell(r, 4).value\n    if not code:\n        continue\n    for c, y in zip(range(8, 13), years):\n        v = ws_data.cell(r, c).value\n        value_map[(code, y)] = v\n\ncomputed_values = {}\n\n# Lookup blocks\nfor r0, r1 in lookup_row_blocks:\n    for r in range(r0, r1 + 1):\n        code = ws_task.cell(r, 4).value\n        for c in range(8, 13):\n            y = ws_task.cell(10, c).value\n            computed_values[f\"{get_column_letter(c)}{r}\"] = float(value_map[(code, y)])\n\n# Slack\nfor i in range(6):\n    slack_row = 35 + i\n    fin_row = 12 + i\n    scrap_row = 19 + i\n    cap_row = 26 + i\n    for c in range(8, 13):\n        col = get_column_letter(c)\n        fin = computed_values[f\"{col}{fin_row}\"]\n        scrap = computed_values[f\"{col}{scrap_row}\"]\n        cap = computed_values[f\"{col}{cap_row}\"]\n        computed_values[f\"{col}{slack_row}\"] = (fin - scrap) / cap * 100.0\n\n# Stats\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    col = get_column_letter(c)\n    vals = [computed_values[f\"{col}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    computed_values[f\"{col}42\"] = float(min(vals))\n    computed_values[f\"{col}43\"] = float(max(vals))\n    computed_values[f\"{col}44\"] = float(median(vals))\n    computed_values[f\"{col}45\"] = float(sum(vals) / len(vals))\n    computed_values[f\"{col}46\"] = float(percentile_inc(vals, 0.25))\n    computed_values[f\"{col}47\"] = float(percentile_inc(vals, 0.75))\n\n# Weighted mean\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    col = get_column_letter(c)\n    slacks = [computed_values[f\"{col}{r}\"] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    caps = [computed_values[f\"{col}{r}\"] for r in range(26, 32)]\n    computed_values[f\"{col}50\"] = float(sum(s*w for s, w in zip(slacks, caps)) / sum(caps))\n\n# ------------------------\n# Patch XML\n# ------------------------\nns = {'s': 'xl/' + target\n            break\n\n    sheet_xml = etree.fromstring(zin.read(task_file))\n\n    # Inject cached values\n    for c_el in sheet_xml.findall('.//s:sheetData//s:c', ns):\n        ref = c_el.get('r')\n        if ref not in computed_values:\n            continue\n        f_el = c_el.find('s:f', ns)\n        if f_el is None:\n            continue\n        v_el = c_el.find('s:v', ns)\n        if v_el is None:\n            v_el = etree.Element('{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main}v')\n            # insert after formula element\n            children = list(c_el)\n            try:\n                idx = children.index(f_el)\n                c_el.insert(idx + 1, v_el)\n            except ValueError:\n                c_el.append(v_el)\n        v_el.text = str(computed_values[ref])\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(final_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n        for item in zin.infolist():\n            if item.filename == task_file:\n                zout.writestr(\n                    item,\n                    etree.tostring(sheet_xml, xml_declaration=True, encoding='UTF-8', standalone=True),\n                )\n            else:\n
